[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content='//#############################################################################\n// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.\n// Author: Andreas Olofsson\n// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)\n//#############################################################################\n//\n// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).\n//\n// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight \'w\'. Two independent datapaths\n// share the cell:\n//\n//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads\n//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above\n//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load\n//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.\n//\n//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per\n//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local\n//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one\n//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.\n//\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as\n   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two\'s-complement\n   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1\'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north +\n                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n//#############################################################################\n// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.\n// Author: Andreas Olofsson\n// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)\n//#############################################################################\n//\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//\n// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated\n// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):\n//\n//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a\n//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).\n//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)\n//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).\n//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row\'s psum_north\n//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result\n//     leaving the bottom row.\n//\n// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,\n// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which\n// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.\n//\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,\n   // w_v carries weights south during load.\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1\'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      // the PE mesh\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n//#############################################################################\n// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.\n// Author: Andreas Olofsson\n// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)\n//#############################################################################\n//\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//\n// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands\n// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). Row m of the result, C[m][*]\n//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles\n//      after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input\n// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the\n// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves\n// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the\n// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew\n// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.\n//\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. [ASSISTANT]
Baseline: **area 4604, delay 1004 ps, ADP 4.62M**. The critical path is the weight reg → 8x8 multiplier → 32-bit ripple adder → psum reg. Key optimizations to try:

1. **Narrow accumulators**: max |sum of k products| ≤ 16384k, so per-row psum widths only need 16→19 bits instead of 32 (shorter adder, fewer FFs).
2. **Drop synchronous resets** on all registers except the valid pipeline (safe: vectors load weights before computing; garbage in data-path regs never touches checked outputs since partial sums are born as 0 at the top).
3. Row 0's adder adds a constant 0 → gets optimized away entirely.

Let me write this optimized design:
  -> replace_file(filename='design.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU), optimized.\n//\n// Cycle-accurate with the original logikbench tpu: c_valid = a_valid delayed\n// 2*N-1 compute cycles (compute advances only while w_valid is low), c_data =\n// the A*B result row packed {col N-1 ... col 0}.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the reference RTL (behavior identical):\n//  * Narrow per-row partial sums: a partial sum after k rows is a sum of k\n//    signed DWxDW products, so it fits in $clog2(PMAX*k+1)+1 bits (16..19 for\n//    DW=8,N=8) instead of 32. Shorter adders and far fewer flops.\n//  * Row 0 adds a constant zero partial sum: no adder at all there.\n//  * No resets on datapath registers (partial sums are born as 0 at the top of\n//    each column; input-loaded data is always shifted in before it is consumed,\n//    so register init values never reach a checked output). Only the valid\n//    pipeline is reset, so c_valid is exact from cycle 0.\n//#############################################################################\n\n//#############################################################################\n// PE: stationary weight, activation east, partial sum south (accumulate).\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter WIN  = 1,    // incoming partial-sum width (signed)\n    parameter WOUT = 16)   // outgoing partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                 en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]       w_north,\n    input  [DW-1:0]       a_west,\n    input  [WIN-1:0]      psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [WOUT-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // the only arithmetic cell: one signed DW x DW multiply plus one add\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod      = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire signed [WOUT-1:0] psum_next = $signed(psum_north) + $signed(prod);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east     <= a_west;\n         psum_south <= psum_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N PE mesh. Uniform slot packing: slot(b,j) at [ (b*N+j)*SLOT +: SLOT ]\n//   - psum bands b = 0..N  (band 0 tied to zero, band N = results)\n//   - weight bands b = 0..N (band 0 = w_top, band b+1 = w_south of row b)\n//   - activation slots i*(N+1)+j (j=0 = a_left[i], j+1 = a_east of PE(i,j))\n// Unused slot high bits are pruned by synthesis.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter SLOT = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*SLOT-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   localparam PPMAX = (1 << (DW-1)) * (1 << (DW-1)); // max |product|\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*SLOT-1:0] psum_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0]   w_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0]   a_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      // band 0 of the psum fabric: fresh accumulation (zero)\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign psum_v[j*SLOT +: SLOT] = {SLOT{1'b0}};\n      end\n      // band 0 of the weight fabric: the load port\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // west edge of the activation fabric\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_awest\n         assign a_v[(i*(N+1))*DW +: DW] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // results leave from psum band N\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         assign c_bot[j*SLOT +: SLOT] = psum_v[(N*N+j)*SLOT +: SLOT];\n      end\n      // the mesh\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         // partial sums carry k = i (in) / i+1 (out) products\n         localparam WIN  = $clog2(PPMAX*i     + 1) + 1;\n         localparam WOUT = $clog2(PPMAX*(i+1) + 1) + 1;\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .WIN(WIN), .WOUT(WOUT)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_west     (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[(i*N+j)*SLOT +: WIN]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_east     (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*DW +: DW]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[((i+1)*N+j)*SLOT +: WOUT]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Top: input skew -> systolic array -> output de-skew -> valid pipeline.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input             clk,\n    input             rst,\n    input             w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0] w_data,\n    input             a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0] a_data,\n    output            c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT  = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam SUMW = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i cycles; zeros injected when a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*SUMW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .SLOT(SUMW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles; sign-extend results to\n   // ACCW bits. Every result fits in SUMW bits signed by construction.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [SUMW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*SUMW +: SUMW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-SUMW){cin[SUMW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [SUMW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-SUMW){dr[D-1][SUMW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles. Reset so\n   // c_valid is exact even before the first stream.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Identical result at target_delay=300 — ABC's mapping is insensitive to the constraint here, so structural RTL changes are the lever. The critical path is the fused multiplier + 18-bit carry-propagate adder.

Bold idea: **keep the partial sums in carry-save form** ({s,c} pairs, one full-adder level per PE) and resolve the carry only once at the bottom of each column. This removes the ripple adder from the PE's per-cycle path entirely. Let me implement it as a variant:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Carry-save variant: the vertical partial sums are kept in carry-save form\n// ({s,c} pairs), so each PE performs one CSA level (no carry chain) per cycle;\n// the carry-propagate add happens once at the bottom of each column.\n//\n// Invariant: (s + c) mod 2^AW = the partial sum (two's complement, AW bits).\n// AW = 19 bits for DW=8, N=8 (sum of k<=8 signed products fits signed 19-bit).\n// The psum pair is born as {0,0} at the top of each column, so garbage init\n// values never reach a checked output.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // one CSA level: {s,c} + product -> {s',c'}, c' always even\n   wire [AW-1:0] p = {{(AW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = s_north ^ c_north ^ p;\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((s_north & c_north) | (s_north & p) |\n                           (c_north & p)) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_south <= c_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N PE mesh. Slot packing: slot(b,j) at [ (b*N+j)*SLOT +: SLOT ]\n//   - s/c psum bands b = 0..N (band 0 = {0,0}, band N = results)\n//   - weight bands b = 0..N (band 0 = w_top, band b+1 = w_south of row b)\n//   - activation slots i*(N+1)+j (j=0 = a_left[i], j+1 = a_east of PE(i,j))\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter AW   = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]   c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v, a_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_awest\n         assign a_v[(i*(N+1))*DW +: DW] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // bottom: one carry-propagate add per column\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] =\n                s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW] + c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_west  (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_east  (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*DW +: DW]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Top: input skew -> systolic array -> output de-skew -> valid pipeline.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam AW  = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){cin[AW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){dr[D-1][AW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_csa.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
CSA design **passes** and improves ADP to **2.84M** (delay 660ps, area 4304). The critical path still contains the multiplier's internal 16-bit carry-propagate adder (the XOR chain + carry cells).

Next bold step: give the multiplier **no CPA at all** — emit its 8 partial-product rows directly and compress everything (product rows + carry-save psum pair) through one explicit CSA tree. The PE path becomes: partial-product gates → 5 CSA levels → registers. Let me build it:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Fully carry-save variant: the product's partial-product rows are never\n// carry-propagated; product rows + the {s,c} psum pair feed one balanced 3:2\n// CSA tree per PE. The only carry-propagate adders are at the bottom of the\n// columns (one per column).\n//\n// Invariant: (s + c) mod 2^AW = partial sum (two's complement, AW=19 bits).\n// Product:  a*w = sum_{r=0..6} a[r]*w*2^r - a[7]*w*2^7\n//   rows 0..6:  a[r] ? (w_sext << r) : 0\n//   row  7   :  a[7] ? ~(w_sext << 7) : 0      (=-x-1)\n//   row  8   :  a[7]                            (the +1 correction at bit 0)\n//   rows 9,10:  s_north, c_north\n// 12 slots (row 11 = 0) -> 8 -> 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 = {s_next, c_next}.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws = $signed(w);\n\n   // ---- partial-product rows ------------------------------------------------\n   wire [AW-1:0] pp [0:11];\n   genvar r;\n   generate\n      for (r = 0; r < 7; r = r + 1) begin : g_pp\n         assign pp[r] = a_west[r] ? (ws << r) : {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign pp[7]  = a_west[7] ? ~(ws << 7) : {AW{1'b0}};\n   assign pp[8]  = {{(AW-1){1'b0}}, a_west[7]};\n   assign pp[9]  = s_north;\n   assign pp[10] = c_north;\n   assign pp[11] = {AW{1'b0}};\n\n   // ---- balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 12 -> 8 -> 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 -----------------\n   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:7];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:5];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l3 [0:3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l4 [0:2];\n\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_l1\n         assign l1[2*k]   = pp[3*k] ^ pp[3*k+1] ^ pp[3*k+2];\n         assign l1[2*k+1] = ((pp[3*k] & pp[3*k+1]) | (pp[3*k] & pp[3*k+2]) |\n                             (pp[3*k+1] & pp[3*k+2])) << 1;\n      end\n      for (k = 0; k < 2; k = k + 1) begin : g_l2\n         assign l2[2*k]   = l1[3*k] ^ l1[3*k+1] ^ l1[3*k+2];\n         assign l2[2*k+1] = ((l1[3*k] & l1[3*k+1]) | (l1[3*k] & l1[3*k+2]) |\n                             (l1[3*k+1] & l1[3*k+2])) << 1;\n      end\n      assign l2[4] = l1[6];\n      assign l2[5] = l1[7];\n      for (k = 0; k < 2; k = k + 1) begin : g_l3\n         assign l3[2*k]   = l2[3*k] ^ l2[3*k+1] ^ l2[3*k+2];\n         assign l3[2*k+1] = ((l2[3*k] & l2[3*k+1]) | (l2[3*k] & l2[3*k+2]) |\n                             (l2[3*k+1] & l2[3*k+2])) << 1;\n      end\n      assign l4[0] = l3[0] ^ l3[1] ^ l3[2];\n      assign l4[1] = ((l3[0] & l3[1]) | (l3[0] & l3[2]) |\n                      (l3[1] & l3[2])) << 1;\n      assign l4[2] = l3[3];\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l4[0] ^ l4[1] ^ l4[2];\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l4[0] & l4[1]) | (l4[0] & l4[2]) |\n                           (l4[1] & l4[2])) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_south <= c_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N PE mesh (same packing as design_csa).\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]   c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v, a_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_awest\n         assign a_v[(i*(N+1))*DW +: DW] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] =\n                s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW] + c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_west  (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .a_east  (a_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*DW +: DW]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Top: input skew -> systolic array -> output de-skew -> valid pipeline.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam AW  = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){cin[AW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){dr[D-1][AW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_csa2.sv')

Best so far: 2827095.97300741 area_delay_product (eval 5)

[ASSISTANT]
ADP 2.83M — delay improved to 559ps but area grew to 5054 (my CSA tree is less area-efficient than ABC's multiplier). Net wash. 

Better idea: **radix-4 Booth-recode the activation** into 4 signed digits {-2..2} (carried through the A-path as digit bundles), so each PE needs only **4 term rows + correction + psum pair = 7 rows → 4 CSA levels** instead of 12 rows → 5 levels. Fewer rows = less area *and* less depth:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Booth variant: the activation stream is radix-4 Booth recoded once at the\n// input (digit bundle {t1,t2,neg} x 4 shifts through the skew/a_east chains),\n// so each PE forms its product as 4 signed term rows\n//     T_k = (neg ? -1 : 1) * (t1 ? w<<2k : (t2 ? w<<(2k+1) : 0))\n// and compresses {T0..T3, corr, s_north, c_north} (7 rows) with one balanced\n// 3:2 CSA tree (4 levels). The only carry-propagate adders are at the bottom\n// of the columns (one per column).\n//\n//   a = sum_k d_k 4^k,  d_k = a[2k] + a[2k-1] - 2 a[2k+1] in {-2..2}\n//   product = sum_k d_k * (w_sext << 2k)\n//   corr = sum_k neg_k (0..4) at bit 0  (each -x == ~x + 1)\n//\n// Invariant: (s + c) mod 2^AW = partial sum (two's complement, AW = 19 bits).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [11:0]       d_west,     // Booth digit bundle of the activation\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [11:0]   d_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws = $signed(w);\n\n   // ---- 4 Booth term rows + correction row + psum pair ---------------------\n   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:6];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_term\n         wire [AW-1:0] sel =\n              ((ws << (2*k))     & {AW{d_west[3*k]}}) |\n              ((ws << (2*k + 1)) & {AW{d_west[3*k+1]}});\n         assign row[k] = sel ^ {AW{d_west[3*k+2]}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   wire [3:0] cnt4 = {3'b0, d_west[2]} + {3'b0, d_west[5]} +\n                     {3'b0, d_west[8]} + {3'b0, d_west[11]};\n   assign row[4] = {{(AW-3){1'b0}}, cnt4[2:0]};\n   assign row[5] = s_north;\n   assign row[6] = c_north;\n\n   // ---- balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 7 -> 5 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 ----------------------\n   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:2];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l3 [0:1];\n\n   assign l1[0] = row[0] ^ row[1] ^ row[2];\n   assign l1[1] = ((row[0] & row[1]) | (row[0] & row[2]) |\n                   (row[1] & row[2])) << 1;\n   assign l1[2] = row[3] ^ row[4] ^ row[5];\n   assign l1[3] = ((row[3] & row[4]) | (row[3] & row[5]) |\n                   (row[4] & row[5])) << 1;\n\n   assign l2[0] = l1[0] ^ l1[1] ^ l1[2];\n   assign l2[1] = ((l1[0] & l1[1]) | (l1[0] & l1[2]) |\n                   (l1[1] & l1[2])) << 1;\n   assign l2[2] = row[6];\n\n   assign l3[0] = l2[0] ^ l2[1] ^ l2[2];\n   assign l3[1] = ((l2[0] & l2[1]) | (l2[0] & l2[2]) |\n                   (l2[1] & l2[2])) << 1;\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l3[0] ^ l3[1] ^ row[6];\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l3[0] & l3[1]) | (l3[0] & row[6]) |\n                           (l3[1] & row[6])) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         d_east  <= d_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_south <= c_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N PE mesh. Slot packing as before; digit bundles flow west->east,\n// weights south, {s,c} psum pairs south.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*12-1:0]   d_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]   c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*12-1:0] d_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dwest\n         assign d_v[(i*(N+1))*12 +: 12] = d_left[i*12 +: 12];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] =\n                s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW] + c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_west  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Top: Booth recode -> input skew -> systolic array -> de-skew -> valid pipe.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam AW  = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // radix-4 Booth recode of an activation element\n   function [11:0] booth (input [DW-1:0] a);\n      integer k;\n      reg y, y1, y2;\n      begin\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            y  = a[2*k];\n            y1 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            y2 = a[2*k+1];\n            booth[3*k]   = y ^ y1;\n            booth[3*k+1] = (y1 & y & ~y2) | (~y1 & ~y & y2);\n            booth[3*k+2] = y2 & ~(y1 & y);\n         end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // input skew on digit bundles\n   wire [N*12-1:0] d_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [11:0] din = a_valid ? booth(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 12'b0;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){cin[AW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){dr[D-1][AW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_booth.sv')

**ADP 2.558M** — the Booth variant wins (area 4526, delay 565). Now let me squeeze further with two refinements:
1. XOR-form term rows (disjoint masks → flatter mapping into the CSA XORs)

[ASSISTANT]
Interesting — booth2 is *worse* (area grew; XOR-form terms + bias adder cost more). `design_booth` remains best at 2.558M.

Key insight for a bigger win: apply the negation **to the base** instead of complementing the term row — store `-w` alongside `w` (computed at load). Then no `+1` correction row is needed at all: **6 rows → 3 CSA levels** (one full level less), and the correction-count logic disappears:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Booth variant v3:\n//  * activation radix-4 Booth recoded once at the input ({t1,t2,neg} x 4);\n//  * each PE stores w and -w (9-bit, computed at load), so a negative digit\n//    negates the BASE (not the term row): no +1 corrections, no extra rows;\n//  * each PE: 4 term rows + psum pair = 6 rows, one balanced 3:2 CSA tree\n//    (3 levels); only the column-bottom CPAs propagate carries.\n//\n//   a = sum_k d_k 4^k,  d_k = a[2k] + a[2k-1] - 2 a[2k+1] in {-2..2}\n//   T_k = |d_k| * ((neg ? -w : w) << 2k),  product = sum_k T_k\n//\n// Invariant: (s + c) mod 2^AW = partial sum (two's complement, AW = 19).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [11:0]       d_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [11:0]   d_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0]   w;\n   reg [DW:0]     negw;                 // -w, signed, 9 bits (w=-128 case)\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws  = $signed(w);\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] wns = $signed(negw);\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:5];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_term\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] wsel = d_west[3*k+2] ? wns : ws;\n         assign row[k] = ((wsel << (2*k))     & {AW{d_west[3*k]}}) |\n                         ((wsel << (2*k + 1)) & {AW{d_west[3*k+1]}});\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign row[4] = s_north;\n   assign row[5] = c_north;\n\n   // balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2\n   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:1];\n\n   assign l1[0] = row[0] ^ row[1] ^ row[2];\n   assign l1[1] = ((row[0] & row[1]) | (row[0] & row[2]) |\n                   (row[1] & row[2])) << 1;\n   assign l1[2] = row[3] ^ row[4] ^ row[5];\n   assign l1[3] = ((row[3] & row[4]) | (row[3] & row[5]) |\n                   (row[4] & row[5])) << 1;\n\n   assign l2[0] = l1[0] ^ l1[1] ^ l1[2];\n   assign l2[1] = ((l1[0] & l1[1]) | (l1[0] & l1[2]) |\n                   (l1[1] & l1[2])) << 1;\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l2[0] ^ l2[1] ^ l1[3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l2[0] & l2[1]) | (l2[0] & l1[3]) |\n                           (l2[1] & l1[3])) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w    <= w_north;\n         negw <= -$signed({w_north[DW-1], w_north});\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         d_east  <= d_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_south <= c_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N PE mesh: digit bundles west->east, weights south, {s,c} pairs south.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*12-1:0]   d_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]   c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*12-1:0] d_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = =\n                s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW] + c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_west .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n pipe.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam AW  = $clog2((((1<<(DW-1))*(1<<(DW-1)))*N) + 1) + 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   function [11:0] booth (input [DW-1:0] a);\n      integer k;\n      reg y, y1, y2;\n      begin\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            y  = a[2*k];\n            y1 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            y2 = a[2*k+1];\n            booth[3*k]   = y ^ y1;\n            booth[3*k+1] = (y1 & y & ~y2) | (~y1 & ~y & y2);\n            booth[3*k+2] = y2 & ~(y1 & y);\n         end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [N*12-1:0] d_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [11:0] din = a_valid ? booth(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 12'b0;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n
