[ASSISTANT]
**ADP 2.240M!** Huge win — storing `-w` at load killed the correction row *and* a CSA level (area 4379, delay 512). The critical path is now `w` → select/mask → 3 CSA levels.

Let me squeeze more: apply the **18-bit offset-form** psum pairs to booth3 (narrower tree & registers, bias folded into the bottom adder):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Booth variant v4 = v3 + offset-form psum pairs:\n//  * activation radix-4 Booth recoded once at the input ({t1,t2,neg} x 4);\n//  * each PE stores w and -w (9-bit, computed at load): negative digits negate\n//    the BASE, so term rows need no +1 corrections;\n//  * 4 term rows + psum pair = 6 rows, one balanced 3:2 CSA tree (3 levels);\n//  * psum pairs store (partial sum + OFF) mod 2^AW with AW = 18 unsigned bits\n//    (OFF = 16256*N); the offset is removed by a constant bias in the single\n//    bottom carry-propagate adder per column (19-bit signed result).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 18)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [11:0]       d_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [11:0]   d_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   reg [DW:0]   negw;                  // -w, signed 9 bits (w = -128 case)\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws  = $signed(w);\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] wns = $signed(negw);\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:5];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_term\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] wsel = d_west[3*k+2] ? wns : ws;\n         assign row[k] = ((wsel << (2*k))     & {AW{d_west[3*k]}}) |\n                         ((wsel << (2*k + 1)) & {AW{d_west[3*k+1]}});\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign row[4] = s_north;\n   assign row[5] = c_north;\n\n   // balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2\n   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:1];\n\n   assign l1[0] = row[0] ^ row[1] ^ row[2];\n   assign l1[1] = ((row[0] & row[1]) | (row[0] & row[2]) |\n                   (row[1] & row[2])) << 1;\n   assign l1[2] = row[3] ^ row[4] ^ row[5];\n   assign l1[3] = ((row[3] & row[4]) | (row[3] & row[5]) |\n                   (row[4] & row[5])) << 1;\n\n   assign l2[0] = l1[0] ^ l1[1] ^ l1[2];\n   assign l2[1] = ((l1[0] & l1[1]) | (l1[0] & l1[2]) |\n                   (l1[1] & l1[2])) << 1;\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l2[0] ^ l2[1] ^ l1[3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l2[0] & l2[1]) | (l2[0] & l1[3]) |\n                           (l2[1] & l1[3])) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w    <= w_north;\n         negw <= -$signed({w_north[DW-1], w_north});\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         d_east  <= d_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_south <= c_next;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N   = 8,\n    parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 18,\n    parameter OFF = 130048)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*12-1:0]     d_left,\n    output [N*(AW+1)-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*12-1:0] d_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = OFF[AW-1:0];     // offset-form zero\n         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dwest\n         assign d_v[(i*(N+1))*12 +: 12] = d_left[i*12 +: 12];\n      end\n      // bottom: one carry-propagate adder per column; removes the offset\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         localparam BIAS = (1 << (AW+1)) - OFF;\n         wire [AW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         wire [AW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         assign c_bot[j*(AW+1) +: (AW+1)] = {1'b0, sv + cv} + BIAS[AW:0];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_west  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT   = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PPMAX = (1 << (DW-1)) * (1 << (DW-1));\n   localparam PPMIN = (1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1);\n   localparam OFF   = PPMIN * N;\n   localparam AW    = $clog2(OFF + PPMAX*N + 1);      // 18\n   localparam SUMW  = AW + 1;                          // 19-bit signed\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   function [11:0] booth (input [DW-1:0] a);\n      integer k;\n      reg y, y1, y2;\n      begin\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            y  = a[2*k];\n            y1 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            y2 = a[2*k+1];\n            booth[3*k]   = y ^ y1;\n            booth[3*k+1] = (y1 & y & ~y2) | (~y1 & ~y & y2);\n            booth[3*k+2] = y2 & ~(y1 & y);\n         end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [N*12-1:0] d_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [11:0] din = a_valid ? booth(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 12'b0;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*SUMW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW), .OFF(OFF)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [SUMW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*SUMW +: SUMW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-SUMW){cin[SUMW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [SUMW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-SUMW){dr[D-1][SUMW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_booth4.sv')

j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, cin} - OFF;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, dr[D-1]} - OFF;\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_booth5.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
The gate formulation is worse (2.467M) — revert to booth4. Now a **clever trick to kill the enable muxes on the 36 psum bits/PE**: during a stall the pair may legally transform to *any* pair with the same sum (e.g. `CSA(s,c,0)`) since only the sum is architecturally visible! So gate the term rows with `en` (free — fold into the scalar digit conditions) and let the psum registers run unconditionally:

[ASSISTANT]
Found the bug! During a stall, `s_south <= s_next` makes the psum **move south** (s_next comes from `s_north` = the row above's register) — the value must come from the PE's **own** register. Fix: mux the tree's psum inputs between `s_north` (compute) and the register's own `s_south` (stall — re-encode of *its own* sum). This keeps the FFs unconditional while being semantically correct:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_booth5.sv', new_string="   // During a stall (en=0) the tree is fed the PE's OWN pair, so the output\n   // is a re-encoding of its own sum (terms are gated off); during compute it\n   // is fed the pair from the row above. Hence the s/c registers are\n   // unconditional -- no enable muxes on 2*AW flops per PE.\n   assign row[4] = en ? s_north : s_south;\n   assign row[5] = en ? c_north : c_south;", old_string='   assign row[4] = s_north;\n   assign row[5] = c_north;')

[Step 34/60]
//#############################################################################
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).
// Booth variant v4 = v3 + offset-form psum pairs:
//  * activation radix-4 Booth recoded once at the input ({t1,t2,neg} x 4);
//  * each PE stores w and -w (9-bit, computed at load): negative digits negate
//    the BASE, so term rows need no +1 corrections;
//  * 4 term rows + psum pair = 6 rows, one balanced 3:2 CSA tree (3 levels);
//  * psum pairs store (partial sum + OFF) mod 2^AW with AW = 18 unsigned bits
//    (OFF = 16256*N); the offset is removed by a constant bias in the single
//    bottom carry-propagate adder per column (19-bit signed result).
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW  = 8,
    parameter AW  = 18)
   (
    input               clk,
    input               ld,
    input               en,
    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,
    input  [11:0]       d_west,
    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,
    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,
    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,
    output reg [11:0]   d_east,
    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,
    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south
    );

   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   reg [DW:0]   negw;                  // -w, signed 9 bits (w = -128 case)
   assign w_south = w;

   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws  = $signed(w);
   wire signed [AW-1:0] wns = $signed(negw);

   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:5];
   genvar k;
   generate
      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_term
         wire signed [AW-1:0] wsel = d_west[3*k+2] ? wns : ws;
         assign row[k] = ((wsel << (2*k))     & {AW{d_west[3*k]}}) |
                         ((wsel << (2*k + 1)) & {AW{d_west[3*k+1]}});
      end
   endgenerate
   assign row[4] = s_north;
   assign row[5] = c_north;

   // balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2
   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:3];
   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:1];

   assign l1[0] = row[0] ^ row[1] ^ row[2];
   assign l1[1] = ((row[0] & row[1]) | (row[0] & row[2]) |
                   (row[1] & row[2])) << 1;
   assign l1[2] = row[3] ^ row[4] ^ row[5];
   assign l1[3] = ((row[3] & row[4]) | (row[3] & row[5]) |
                   (row[4] & row[5])) << 1;

   assign l2[0] = l1[0] ^ l1[1] ^ l1[2];
   assign l2[1] = ((l1[0] & l1[1]) | (l1[0] & l1[2]) |
                   (l1[1] & l1[2])) << 1;

   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l2[0] ^ l2[1] ^ l1[3];
   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l2[0] & l2[1]) | (l2[0] & l1[3]) |
                           (l2[1] & l1[3])) << 1;

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (ld) begin
         w    <= w_north;
         negw <= -$signed({w_north[DW-1], w_north});
      end
      if (en) begin
         d_east  <= d_west;
         s_south <= s_next;
         c_south <= c_next;
      end
   end

endmodule

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N   = 8,
    parameter DW  = 8,
    parameter AW  = 18,
    parameter OFF = 130048)
   (
    input                 clk,
    input                 ld,
    input                 en,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,
    input  [N*12-1:0]     d_left,
    output [N*AW-1:0]     c_bot
    );

   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;
   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v;
   wire [N*(N+1)*12-1:0] d_v;

   genvar i, j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top
         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = OFF[AW-1:0];     // offset-form zero
         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};
      end
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop
         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
      end
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dwest
         assign d_v[(i*(N+1))*12 +: 12] = d_left[i*12 +: 12];
      end
      // bottom: one carry-propagate adder per column (offset form kept)
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot
         wire [AW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];
         wire [AW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];
         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = sv + cv;
      end
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe
              (.clk     (clk),
               .ld      (ld),
               .en      (en),
               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),
               .d_west  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*12 +: 12]),
               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),
               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),
               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),
               .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*12 +: 12]),
               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),
               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule

module tpu
  #(parameter N    = 8,
    parameter DW   = 8,
    parameter ACCW = 32)
   (
    input               clk,
    input               rst,
    input               w_valid,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,
    input               a_valid,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,
    output              c_valid,
    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data
    );

   localparam LAT   = 2*N - 1;

... (showing lines 1-153 of 237; use offset/limit for more)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 35/60]
   localparam PPMAX = (1 << (DW-1)) * (1 << (DW-1));
   localparam PPMIN = (1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1);
   localparam OFF   = PPMIN * N;
   localparam AW    = $clog2(OFF + PPMAX*N + 1);      // 18

   wire ld = w_valid;
   wire en = ~w_valid;

   function [11:0] booth (input [DW-1:0] a);
      integer k;
      reg y, y1, y2;
      begin
         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin
            y  = a[2*k];
            y1 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];
            y2 = a[2*k+1];
            booth[3*k]   = y ^ y1;
            booth[3*k+1] = (y1 & y & ~y2) | (~y1 & ~y & y2);
            booth[3*k+2] = y2 & ~(y1 & y);
         end
      end
   endfunction

   wire [N*12-1:0] d_left;
   genvar i, j;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [11:0] din = a_valid ? booth(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 12'b0;
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [11:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW), .OFF(OFF)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, cin} - OFF;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =
                   {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, dr[D-1]} - OFF;
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dwest\n         assign d_v[(i*(N+1))*12 +: 12] = d_left[i*12 +: 12];\n      end\n      // bottom: one carry-propagate adder per column (offset form kept)\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         wire [AW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         wire [AW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = sv + cv;\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_west  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT   = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PPMAX = (1 << (DW-1)) * (1 << (DW-1));\n   localparam PPMIN = (1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1);\n   localparam OFF   = PPMIN * N;\n   localparam AW    = $clog2(OFF + PPMAX*N + 1);      // 18\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   function [11:0] booth (input [DW-1:0] a);\n      integer k;\n      reg y, y1, y2;\n      begin\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            y  = a[2*k];\n            y1 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            y2 = a[2*k+1];\n            booth[3*k]   = y ^ y1;\n            booth[3*k+1] = (y1 & y & ~y2) | (~y1 & ~y & y2);\n            booth[3*k+2] = y2 & ~(y1 & y);\n         end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [N*12-1:0] d_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [11:0] din = a_valid ? booth(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 12'b0;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign d_left[i*12 +: 12] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW), .OFF(OFF)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, cin} - OFF;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, dr[D-1]} - OFF;\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design.sv')

Now a more principled variant: the recoding `a = Σ_{k≤2}(s0−2s1)ₖ4ᵏ + d₃·64` gives **2-bit digits with no negation-mux for rows 0–2** (the sign is baked into the digit: `+w` for s0, `−w` for s1) and a **9-bit digit bundle** (was 12). Only the top digit keeps a mux:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Booth variant v7: mixed 2-bit signed digits -- mux-free term rows for k=0..2\n//   a = sum_{k=0..2} (s0_k - 2 s1_k) 4^k  +  d3 * 64,  d3 in {-2..2}\n//   term_k = s0_k*(w<<2k) + s1_k*((-w)<<(2k+1))          (no base mux)\n//   term_3 = (t1 + 2 t2)*(neg ? -w : w) << 6\n// Digit bundle is 9 bits (was 12). 6 rows -> 3-level CSA tree, psum pair in\n// offset form (AW = 18), offset removed on the output side.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 18)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [8:0]        d_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [8:0]    d_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   reg [DW:0]   negw;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws  = $signed(w);\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] wns = $signed(negw);\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:5];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; [ASSISTANT]
v7 has a bug: the 2-bit digit `(s0,s1)` can have **both bits set** (d=−1), so OR-ing the masked bases is invalid (OR≠ADD). Fix: use **one-hot digits** per position — mutually exclusive masks make the OR correct, and *all* rows become mux-free:

[ASSISTANT]
**ADP 2.030M** (area 4203, delay 483) — best yet. Let me consolidate this into `design.sv`:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Best design: one-hot signed-digit recode + carry-save psum pairs.\n//  * a recoded once at the input: a = sum_{k<=2} d_k 4^k + d3*64,\n//    d_k in {0,1,-1,-2}, d3 in {-2..2}; digits are ONE-HOT (p1,n1,n2)/(p1,p2,\n//    n1,n2) so masked bases combine with OR (== ADD, masks exclusive) and no\n//    per-digit base mux is needed;\n//  * each PE stores w and -w (9-bit, computed at load);\n//  * 4 term rows + psum pair = 6 rows, one balanced 3:2 CSA tree (3 levels);\n//  * psum pairs store (partial sum + OFF) mod 2^AW, AW = 18 unsigned bits\n//    (OFF = 16256*N); offset removed by a constant subtract on the output;\n//  * carry row stored shifted right one (its LSB is always 0).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 18)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,\n    input  [12:0]       d_west,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     s_north,\n    input  [AW-1:0]     c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,\n    output reg [12:0]   d_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0] s_south,\n    output [AW-1:0]     c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   reg [DW:0]   negw;\n   reg [AW-2:0] c_hi;                  // c_south >> 1 (LSB is always 0)\n   assign w_south = w;\n   assign c_south = {c_hi, 1'b0};\n\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] ws  = $signed(w);\n   wire signed [AW-1:0] wns = $signed(negw);\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] row [0:5];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 3; k = k + 1) begin : g_term\n         assign row[k] = ((ws         & {AW{d_west[3*k]}})   |\n                          (wns        & {AW{d_west[3*k+1]}}) |\n                          ((wns << 1) & {AW{d_west[3*k+2]}})) << (2*k);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign row[3] = ((ws         & {AW{d_west[9]}})   |\n                    ((ws  << 1) & {AW{d_west[10]}})  |\n                    (wns        & {AW{d_west[11]}})  |\n                    ((wns << 1) & {AW{d_west[12]}})) << 6;\n   assign row[4] = s_north;\n   assign row[5] = c_north;\n\n   // balanced 3:2 CSA tree: 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2\n   wire [AW-1:0] l1 [0:3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] l2 [0:1];\n\n   assign l1[0] = row[0] ^ row[1] ^ row[2];\n   assign l1[1] = ((row[0] & row[1]) | (row[0] & row[2]) |\n                   (row[1] & row[2])) << 1;\n   assign l1[2] = row[3] ^ row[4] ^ row[5];\n   assign l1[3] = ((row[3] & row[4]) | (row[3] & row[5]) |\n                   (row[4] & row[5])) << 1;\n\n   assign l2[0] = l1[0] ^ l1[1] ^ l1[2];\n   assign l2[1] = ((l1[0] & l1[1]) | (l1[0] & l1[2]) |\n                   (l1[1] & l1[2])) << 1;\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] s_next = l2[0] ^ l2[1] ^ l1[3];\n   wire [AW-1:0] c_next = ((l2[0] & l2[1]) | (l2[0] & l1[3]) |\n                           (l2[1] & l1[3])) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w    <= w_north;\n         negw <= -$signed({w_north[DW-1], w_north});\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         d_east  <= d_west;\n         s_south <= s_next;\n         c_hi    <= c_next[AW-1:1];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N   = 8,\n    parameter DW  = 8,\n    parameter AW  = 18,\n    parameter OFF = 130048)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*13-1:0]     d_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [N*(N+1)*AW-1:0] s_v, c_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*DW-1:0] w_v;\n   wire [N*(N+1)*13-1:0] d_v;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_top\n         assign s_v[j*AW +: AW] = OFF[AW-1:0];\n         assign c_v[j*AW +: AW] = {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wtop\n         assign w_v[j*DW +: DW] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dwest\n         assign d_v[(i*(N+1))*13 +: 13] = d_left[i*13 +: 13];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_bot\n         wire [AW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         wire [AW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+j)*AW +: AW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = sv + cv;\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_west  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j)*13 +: 13]),\n               .s_north (s_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_north (c_v[(i*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .w_south (w_v[((i+1)*N+j)*DW +: DW]),\n               .d_east  (d_v[(i*(N+1)+j+1)*13 +: 13]),\n               .s_south (s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]),\n               .c_south (c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*AW +: AW]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT   = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PPMAX = (1 << (DW-1)) * (1 << (DW-1));\n   localparam PPMIN = (1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1);\n   localparam OFF   = PPMIN * N;\n   localparam AW    = $clog2(OFF + PPMAX*N + 1);\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   function [12:0] recode (input [DW-1:0] a);\n      reg signed [9:0] A0, A1, A2, A3, S;\n      begin\n         A0 = {{(10-DW){a[DW-1]}}, a};\n         S  = A0 >>> 2;\n         A1 = S + {9'b0, A0[1]};\n         S  = A1 >>> 2;\n         A2 = S + {9'b0, A1[1]};\n         S  = A2 >>> 2;\n         A3 = S + {9'b0, A2[1]};\n         // d_k = A[0] - 2*A[1] in {0,1,-1,-2} -> one-hot (p1,n1,n2)\n         recode[0] =  A0[0] & ~A0[1];\n         recode[1] =  A0[0] &  A0[1];\n         recode[2] = ~A0[0] &  A0[1];\n         recode[3] =  A1[0] & ~A1[1];\n         recode[4] =  A1[0] &  A1[1];\n         recode[5] = ~A1[0] &  A1[1];\n         recode[6] =  A2[0] & ~A2[1];\n         recode[7] =  A2[0] &  A2[1];\n         recode[8] = ~A2[0] &  A2[1];\n         // d3 = A3 in {-2..2} -> one-hot (p1,p2,n1,n2)\n         recode[9]  = (A3 ==  1);\n         recode[10] = (A3 ==  2);\n         recode[11] = (A3 == -1);\n         recode[12] = (A3 == -2);\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [N*13-1:0] d_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [12:0] din = a_valid ? recode(a_data[i*DW +: DW]) : 13'b0;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign d_left[i*13 +: 13] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [12:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign d_left[i*13 +: 13] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(AW), .OFF(OFF)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .d_left(d_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, cin} - OFF;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-AW){1'b0}}, dr[D-1]} - OFF;\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design.sv')

The design is complete. Here's a summary of the work:

## Final Result

**`design.sv`** — 100% correctness (6782/6782 cycle-accurate checks, lint clean)
- **ADP: 2,029,401** (area 4202, delay 483 ps) vs. starting point **4,624,409** (area 4604, delay 1004 ps) — a **2.28× improvement**

## Optimization Journey

| Design | Area | Delay | ADP | Key idea |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| starting_point | 4604 | 1004 | 4.62M | reference RTL (32-bit ripple accumulators) |
| design.sv v1 | 4151 | 754 | 3.13M | range-proven narrow accumulators (16–19b), no datapath resets |
| design_csa | 4304 | 660 | 2.84M | carry-save {s,c} psum pairs, CPA only at column bottom |
| design_csa2 | 5054 | 559 | 2.83M | fully fused multiplier-less CSA tree |
| design_booth | 4526 | 565 | 2.56M | radix-4 Booth recode of A at input |
| design_booth3 | 4379 | 512 | 2.24M | store −w at load → no +1 corrections, 6 rows → 3 CSA levels |
| design_booth4 | 4307 | 488 | 2.10M | 18-bit offset-form psums + "shift-last" term rows |
| **design.sv (final)** | **4202** | **483** | **2.03M** | one-hot signed digits → mux-free term rows, `c>>1` storage |

## Key techniques
- **Exact value-range analysis**: sums of k≤8 signed 8×8 products fit 19 bits; offset form (v+130048) fits 18 unsigned bits — the bias is removed by a constant subtract off the reg-to-reg path.
- **Carry-save systolic psums**: each PE is one 3:2 CSA level (6 rows → 2), no carry chains in the array; one carry-propagate adder per column at the bottom.
- **Signed-digit recoding** (`a = Σ dₖ4ᵏ + d₃·64`) done once at the input, carried through the skew/a_east chains; one-hot digits make masked bases OR-combinable with no per-digit mux, and storing `−w` (computed at load) makes negative digits free.
- Preserved exact cycle behavior throughout: 15-cycle latency, `w_valid` stall semantics, zero injection, back-to-back tiles, mid-drain weight reloads.

## Summary

**Final: `design.sv` — PASS, ADP 2.029M** (area 4202, delay 483 ps), vs. `starting_point.v` baseline **4.624M** (area 4604, delay 1004 ps) — a **2.28× improvement**.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Baseline copy** → 4.62M (32-bit ripple accumulate per PE).
- **Narrow accumulators** (value-range proof: Σ of k≤8 signed 8×8 products fits 19 bits; offset form `v+130048` fits 18 unsigned bits) + removing datapath resets → 3.13M.
- **Carry-save psums** (`design_csa.sv`): {s,c} pairs descend the column, one 3:2 CSA level per PE, single CPA at column bottom → 2.84M.
- **Fused multiplier-less CSA tree** (`design_csa2.sv`): 12-row partial-product tree → 2.83M (delay win, area loss).
- **Booth recoding** (`design_booth*.sv`): digits of A recoded once at the input; storing `−w` at load removes all +1 corrections and a whole CSA level (`design_booth3.sv`) → 2.24M; offset-form + "shift-last" term rows (`design_booth4.sv`) → 2.10M.
- **Final (`design_v7.sv`/`design.sv`)**: mixed signed-digit recode (`a = Σdₖ4ᵏ + d₃·64`) with **one-hot digits** so masked bases OR-combine with no per-digit mux, `c>>1` storage → 2.03M.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. Replacing per-PE multiply-accumulate (mult + 18-bit CPA) with **carry-save {s,c} pairs + bottom CPA** — removed all carry chains from the array path.
2. **Storing `−w` at weight-load** — negative Booth digits negate the *base*, killing correction rows/tree levels.
3. **Accumulator width reduction** (32→18/19, offset form) — smaller adders and ~1200 fewer flops.
4. **"Shift-last" term rows** — mask at bit 0, then shift: 513→488 ps.
5. **One-hot digit rows** — mux-free term generation, ~100 area units.

### 3. Regressions / dead ends
- XOR-form term rows, 4-way gate-form rows, mux-after-mask forms — all mapped worse (+150–350 ADP).
- Unconditional psum registers fed from the *north* PE (self-re-encode trick) — **functionally wrong** (psums shift during stalls); fixed self-feedback version was still worse than a plain enable mux.
- Two's-complement digit pairs `(s0,s1)` where both bits can be set — OR≠ADD bug; fixed via one-hot digits.
- `(A0>>>2) + unsigned` silently turned `>>>` into a *logical* shift (mixed signedness) — subtle correctness bug.
- Offset bias folded into the bottom adder created a 2-adder chain that became critical; moved the bias to the output side.
- `target_delay` had zero effect on this flow; some "simplifications" (gate-form, XOR) were strictly worse — mapping lottery matters.

### 4. Lessons / next time
- Gate-level mapping is highly sensitive to *expression shape* (shift placement, OR vs XOR, mux position) even when functions are identical — cheap A/B trials of tiny RTL variants paid off repeatedly.
- Value-range analysis unlocks easy, safe width reduction; offset/bias representations must keep bias math off critical paths.
- Preserve the invariant (here: the *sum* of the carry-save pair) rather than the exact representation — but be careful what "holds" during stalls means structurally.
- Keep a known-good file (`design.sv`) frozen while experimenting in copies (`design_booth*.sv`, `design_v7.sv`); the remaining lever would be shrinking the 13-bit digit bundle / enable muxes on the 36 psum flops per PE.

================================================================================
